# Malcolm Byrne: sponsored Bills, recorded progress and outcomes

**Draft — for review** · Incomplete / qualified research

Original brief: Prepare a briefing on the Bills sponsored by Malcolm Byrne. What progress have they made, and what happened to them?
Reporting date: 2026-11-12

## Recorded sponsorship and progress

The accepted results identify 20 Bills. Recorded status is not a certified final outcome; retrieval scope, freshness and completeness are unverified. [a-ae2ac9537767c26299f9]
_Provisional interpretation — moderate qualitative confidence (provisional)_

Protection of Private Residences (Against Targeted Picketing) Bill 2021 records Fifth Stage and Report Stage (4 July 2024), a Bill Lapsed Event (8 November 2024), then Second Stage (1 May 2025). Seanad Electoral (University Members) (Amendment) Bill 2020 records a Bill Lapsed Event and Committee Stage (29 January 2025), followed by Committee Stage (27 March 2025); the records do not explain these coexisting entries. [a-d5defc65a848c70590fb]
_Provisional interpretation — high qualitative confidence (provisional)_
